### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales sobre una rama que aún no compartiste, y así el historial queda lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está en un remoto compartido o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo se unieron las dos ramas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接的，保证数据按序、完整送达；UDP 是无连接的、不保证送达，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to tidy it up before review, so the history reads as a clean line. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, because rewriting shared history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接指定就行：

```bash
bun test path/to/file.test.ts
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，`bun test foo` 会跑路径里含 `foo` 的测试文件。

要只跑某个测试用例，加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test path/to/file.test.ts -t "用例名称"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data. After a crash, the database replays that log on restart to recover committed changes and keep the data consistent.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着改 compaction 那个 PR 的锁粒度，弄清楚了告诉你。

要做的：
- 读 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁的范围缩小到真正需要保护的部分，不要改动无关的逻辑。
- 在原来的分支上继续提交，保持这个 PR 是草稿，不要合并，也不要推送或改成正式 PR，等用户同意再做。
- 完成标准：测试跑通（给出命令和结果），并说明锁的范围具体怎么改了。
- 不做的事：不改 review 之外的功能，不删除已有测试。
```

完成标准：给出改前改后的增量构建耗时数字，以及跑过的命令输出作为证据。

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海，600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"><div class="k-empty">还没有票价，明天早上会更新。</div></div></div>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return '¥' + n; }
function render(v){
  var trains = (v && v.trains) || [];
  var under = trains.filter(function(t){ return t.price < 600; });
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  if (!trains.length) return;
  count.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  var min = under.length ? Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function(t){ return t.price; })) : null;
  sub.textContent = min === null ? '没有 600 元以下的班次' : '最低 ' + fmt(min);
  list.innerHTML = '';
  trains.forEach(function(t){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no + ' · 二等座余 ' + t.left + ' 张';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = fmt(t.price);
    val.style.color = t.price < 600 ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function(v){ render({trains: v}); });
ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function(v){ render({trains: v}); }); });
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"left":"12"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"left":"4"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"left":"—"}]}

  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1651 characters): <style>
.k-list{margin:0;padding:0;list-style:none}
.k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latestSub">writes/s · p99 latency</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Runs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="runs"></ul>
  </div>
  <script>
    function render(runs) {
      runs = runs || [];
      var list = document.getElementById('runs');
      list.innerHTML = '';
      if (!runs.length) {
        list.innerHTML = '<li class="k-empty">No runs yet. The nightly job will add one here.</li>';
        document.getElementById('latest').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not yet</span>';
        return;
      }
      var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
      document.getElementById('latest').textContent = last.writesPerSec + ' writes/s';
      document.getElementById('latestSub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls + ' · ' + last.night;
      runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
        var li = document.createElement('li');
        li.className = 'k-row';
        li.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div></div><div class="k-row-value">' + r.writesPerSec + ' /s</div>';
        list.appendChild(li);
      });
    }
    render(window.ducter ? null : null);
    if (window.ducter) {
      ducter.get('runs').then(render);
      ducter.onChange(function () { ducter.get('runs').then(render); });
    }
  </script>
</main>

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">还没记</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没有花销</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
</main>
<style>
.k-sum-value{font-size:44px;font-weight:700;color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-sum-label{font-size:16px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-sum-sub{color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-none{color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  const c=document.getElementById("count");
  if(xs.length){
    t.textContent="¥"+total;
    t.classList.remove("k-none");
    c.textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
  }else{
    t.textContent="还没记";
    t.classList.add("k-none");
    c.textContent="还没有花销";
  }
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

      });

  function renderCats() {

      b.type = 'button';
      b.className = 'k-chip';

    if (!(amt > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }

    });

    $('note').value = '';
    $('addbox').open = false;
    save();
  });

  function load(v) {
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }
  function reload() {
    var r = ducter.get('expenses');
    if (r && r.then) r.then(load); else load(r);
  }

  renderCats();
  reload();
  ducter.onChange(reload);
})();

  [an app card under the message: made "宝宝喝奶", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3074 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝奶</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="tot">还没记</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记下一次,这里显示当天总量</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">今天的记录</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>时间</label><input class="k-input" id="t" type="text" placeholder="HH:MM"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="例如 120"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
const $ = i => document.getElementById(i);
let feeds = [], sel = null;
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
function dayKey(){ const d = new Date(); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
function nowHM(){ const d = new Date(); return pad(d.getHours()) + ':' + pad(d.getMinutes()); }

function render(){
  const td = dayKey();
  const today = feeds.filter(f => f.d === td).sort((a … + ' 次,合计 ' + total + 'ml');
  } else {
    $('tot').className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
    $('tot').textContent = '还没记';
    $('sub').textContent = '记下一次,这里显示当天总量';
    $('list').innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">今天还没有记录。</div>';
    ducter.summary('今天还没记');
  }
}

function save(){ ducter.set('feeds', feeds); render(); }

function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(v => { feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; render(); });
}

$('list').onclick = e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) { feeds = feeds.filter(f => f.id !== del.dataset.del); sel = null; save(); return; }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
  if (row) { sel = sel === row.dataset.id ? null : row.dataset.id; render(); }
};

$('add').onclick = () => {
  const raw = ($('t').value || nowHM()).trim();
  const ml = parseFloat($('ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  const p = raw.split(':');
  feeds.push({ id: 'f' + Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), d: dayKey(), t: pad(p[0]) + ':' + p[1], ml: ml });
  $('ml').value = ''; $('t').value = '';
  save();
};

$('t').placeholder = nowHM();
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":"f1","d":"2026-11-23","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":"f2","d":"2026-11-23","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf as "PRs waiting on others". Tick each one as it lands and the count at the top updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2086 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 · tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var prs = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var left = document.getElementById('left');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function draw(done) {
    done = done || {};
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var open = 0;
    prs.forEach(function (p) {
      if (!done[p.id]) open++;
      var row = document.createElement('label');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.style.opacity = done[p.id] ? '0.5' : '1';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<input type="checkbox" class="k-check"' + (done[p.id] ? ' checked' : '') + '>' +
        '<div class="k-row-main"><div></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value"></div>';
      row.querySelector('.k-row-main > div').textContent = p.title;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = p.sub;
      row.querySelector('.k-row-value').textContent = p.link;
      row.querySelector('input').addEventListener('change', function (e) {
        Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (d) {
          d = Object.assign({}, d || {});
          d[p.id] = e.target.checked;
          return Promise.resolve(ducter.set('done', d)).then(function () { draw(d); });
        });
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    left.textContent = open;
    sub.textContent = 'of ' + prs.length + ' · tick each one off as it lands';
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(open + ' waiting, ' + (prs.length - open) + ' landed');
    }
  }

  if (window.ducter) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(draw);
    if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(draw);
    });
  } else {
    draw({});
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut · wey-gu/ducter#412","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Mon","link":"github.com/wey-gu/ducter/pull/412"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502 · nowledge-co/mem#10287","sub":"CI failing","link":"github.com/nowledge-co/mem/pull/10287"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test · nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tue","link":"github.com/nowledge-co/hawdb/pull/1592"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar · wey-gu/ducter#418","sub":"Draft","link":"github.com/wey-gu/ducter/pull/418"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

.k-app{font-family:inherit;color:var(--d-ink);background:var(--d-bg);padding:16px … torAll('.k-chip').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {

  document.querySelectorAll('.k-chip').forEach(x => x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(x === b)));
});

function draw() {
  const xs = ducter.get('items') || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.amt || 0), 0);
  $('sum').className = xs.length ? 'k-sum-value' : 'k-sum-value k-none';
  $('sum').textContent = xs.length ? fmt(total) : '还没记';
  $('sumsub').textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + ' 笔' : '记下第一笔花销';
  $('list').innerHTML = xs.length
    ? xs.map(x => '<div class="k-row"><div><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(x.trip) + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(x.day) + ' · ' + esc(x.cat) + '</div></div><div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(x.amt) + '</div></div>').join('')
    : '<p class="k-empty">暂无花销</p>';
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(total) : '还没记');
}

$('save').onclick = () => {
  const amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!amt) return;
  const xs = ducter.get('items') || [];
  xs.push({ trip: $('trip').value.trim(), day: $('day').value.trim(), cat, amt });
  ducter.set('items', xs);
  $('amt').value = '';
  draw();
};

ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 高铁 上海虹桥→北京南","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}
